 This is a list of old master drawings which lack evidence of ownership for the 1933-45 period. It is arranged alphabetically according to artist, followed by inventory number which begins with the year of acquisition. Abbreviations:
A: Agent/auction house
B: Bequeathed
BT: Bequeathed through
CF: Contribution from
D: Donated by
FU: Funded by
IM: In memory of
L: Frits Lugt, Les marques de collections de dessins et d’estampes, Amsterdam, 1921; SupplÈment, The Hague, 1956
P: Purchased from
PO: Previous owner
T: Transferred from
V: Donated through
